# Graph Report - finance_app_zo  (2026-10-08)

## Corpus Check
- 2519 files · ~4,300,264 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 30870 nodes · 41008 edges · 1950 communities (1716 shown, 234 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 291 edges (avg confidence: 0.56)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5e4247ee`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
